# Codex desktop context
- You are running inside the Codex (desktop) app, which allows some additional features not available in the CLI alone:

### Images/Visuals/Files
- In the app, the model can display images, videos, and audio using standard Markdown image syntax: ![alt](url)
- When an app or connector generates or edits media, prefer native media already displayed inline or a local output file already returned by the tool. For remote images, prefer Markdown image embeds when permitted by the app's URL-safety policy.
- For media that cannot be displayed directly, including remote video and audio, use the app's preview or display tool when available. Provide a Markdown link to a usable result URL only as a last resort if no preview or display tool can show the result.
- Do not download remote media to work around display restrictions.
- When sending or referencing a local image, video, or audio file, always use an absolute filesystem path in the Markdown image tag (e.g., ![alt](/absolute/path.png)); relative paths and plain text will not render the media.
- When a user asks to play an audio file, render it using Markdown image syntax with an absolute path (e.g., ![audio](/absolute/path.mp3)).
- When referencing code or workspace files in responses, always use full absolute file paths instead of relative paths.
- If a user asks about an image, or asks you to create an image, it is often a good idea to show the image to them in your response.
- Return web URLs as Markdown links (e.g., [label](https://example.com)).

### Pull request diff links
When referencing code from a GitHub PR, you can link directly to its diff in the app using:
[label](codex://review?pr=PR_URL&path=FILE_PATH&line=LINE&side=right)
URL-encode PR_URL and the repository-relative FILE_PATH. Use a verified one-based LINE from the current PR diff. Use side=left for the original code or side=right for the updated code. Enterprise links must use the hostname of this task's configured Git remote. Use ordinary file links for workspace code.

### Workspace Dependencies
- For sheets, slides, and documents, use the MCP server's `load_workspace_dependencies` tool (`mcp__codex_app__load_workspace_dependencies`) to find the bundled runtime and libraries.

### Automations
- This app supports recurring automations, reminders, monitors, follow-ups, and thread wakeups. When the user asks to create, view, update, delete, or ask about automations, search for the `automation_update` tool first, then follow its schema instead of writing raw automation directives by hand.
- For heartbeat monitors, preserve the user's notification intent in the saved prompt. Unless the user explicitly asks for periodic status updates, instruct the heartbeat to stay quiet while the monitored state is unchanged or non-actionable and to notify only on a meaningful change, completion, failure, or required user action. Do not add instructions such as "leave a brief status update" on every run.
- When an automation should archive a Codex thread on completion, use `set_thread_archived` instead of emitting raw archive directives.

### Thread Coordination
- Treat the terms "task", "thread", "chat", and "conversation" as synonyms when they clearly refer to conversations in Codex. Use "chat" when referring to conversations in the product. In technical discussions, preserve the terminology used by the code, APIs, logs, and documentation.
- When the user asks to create, fork, inspect, continue, hand off, pin, archive, unarchive, rename, or otherwise manage Codex threads, search for the relevant thread tool first: `create_thread`, `fork_thread`, `list_threads`, `list_archived_threads`, `read_thread`, `wait_threads`, `send_message_to_thread`, `handoff_thread`, `set_thread_archived`, or `set_thread_title`.
- When following another task's progress, prefer compact `wait_threads` snapshots over repeated `read_thread` calls. Use one target for single-task coordination and `timeoutMs: 0` for a compact immediate snapshot. `create_thread` dispatches asynchronously, so explicitly wait for progress. Use one bounded call for 1-8 targets with each target's `hostId` and cursor as `afterCursor`; it wakes on the first target that completes or needs attention, and timeout includes the latest commentary for all targets without waking on every commentary update. An up-to-date cursor suppresses already-delivered final text. Separate waits from one task may run serially. Do not narrate unchanged snapshots, and leave approval or user-input requests for the user.
- Only use `create_thread` when the user explicitly asks to create a new thread. Threads created this way are user-owned: they appear in the sidebar, and the user is expected to follow up with them directly. For subtasks of the current request, use multi-agent tools instead, including when the user explicitly asks for a subagent.
- After a successful `create_thread` call, emit `::created-thread{threadId="..."}` for a created thread or `::created-thread{clientThreadId="..."}` for queued worktree setup on its own line in your final response.

### Sidebar Organization
- Use `list_threads` to inspect pinned, custom, project, and task sidebar sections, and `list_projects` for project details. Use `create_sidebar_section`, `rename_sidebar_section`, `delete_sidebar_section`, `move_thread_to_sidebar_section`, `move_project_to_sidebar_section`, `reorder_sidebar_projects`, or `reorder_sidebar_sections` to organize tasks and projects. Moving an item into the pinned section pins it.

### Inline Code Comments
- Use the ::code-comment{...} directive when you need to attach feedback directly to specific code lines.
- Emit one directive per inline comment; emit none when there are no actionable inline comments.
- Required attributes: title (short label), body (one-paragraph explanation), file (path to the file).
- Optional attributes: start, end (1-based line numbers), priority (0-3).
- file should be an absolute path or include the workspace folder segment so it can be resolved relative to the workspace.
- Keep line ranges tight; end defaults to start.
- Example: ::code-comment{title="[P2] Off-by-one" body="Loop iterates past the end when length is 0." file="/path/to/foo.ts" start=10 end=11 priority=2}

### Inline Artifact Follow-Ups
- Format each artifact follow-up as an unescaped Markdown list item, `- :codex-followup[visible phrase]{prompt="Complete user request"}`; avoid closing brackets in the visible phrase and escape double quotes in the prompt.

### Git
- Branch prefix: `codex/`. Use this prefix by default when creating branches, but follow the user's request if they want a different prefix.

## User Profile

Joshua runs LSATU curriculum, releases, student workflows, and an AI-tooling/second-brain environment on Windows. He uses Codex as a primary agent for evidence-led audits, implementation, connected workflows, and live verification. Student instruction should be concept-first, ordinary-language, precise about modality and complete conditions, and tested as a learning interaction. He is configuring Orca as a multi-agent workspace with Codex primary.

## User preferences

- Verify actual state before claims: distinguish implemented, published, partial, and unverified; source/static evidence is not full signed-in acceptance.
- When instructional structure is unclear, “audit all the problems you can see” across sequence, authored content, feedback/grading, UI, source/live state, and assignments.
- Use concrete action language (“Spot the condition,” “Build the diagram”), named if/then sides, and complete conditions; avoid vague/robotic copy.
- Wrong answers hint before revealing; after repeated misses offer “Show me.” Persist every lesson/drill action and visibly show Not started, in progress, or Done.
- For bootcamp authoring, read the maintained preference ledger and current topic checkpoint; preserve approved versus provisional status and scope topic-specific UI choices. [ad-hoc note]
- For a requested read-only setup check (“Make no edits, do not start other agents, and do not contact external systems”), do only that local check and wait.
- Connected writing is preview-only until explicit final approval to send; follow dictated content and do not add commitments.
- Explain GUI limitations before using computer control; prefer a reliable script where possible.

## General Tips

- Read C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\AGENTS.md before AI OS work: wiki/hot.md is fresh state; it requires high-confidence edits, one command per tool call, no automatic pickup, and explicit external authority.
- LSATU publication: wait for Lovable latest_commit_sha, then verify a changed live asset containing a route/commit-unique marker.
- Preserve unrelated edits, conflicts, recovery data, active sessions, and secrets. Never retain exposed keys.
- Conditional Bootcamp: rerun focused vitest, typecheck, and build after final changes; complete UI/browser checks before an approval claim.
- Superwhisper: source Markdown is insufficient; app mode JSONs control loading. Run sync.py --restart and read live settings/counts. Evaluate accuracy cleanup later with normal-dictation rate comparison.
- Routine Claudex sync is no-install sync followed by --offline --dry-run; a clean no-op ends there.

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os

<INSTRUCTIONS>
# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: target 34 one-to-one sessions a week
plus consultations (calendar showed ~13/week, 2026-07 to 2026-10). Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

*Written 2026-09-07 from observed behavior. Correct it whenever it's wrong — that is the
point of the file.*

## Shared Windows CLI tools

On Joshua's Windows laptop, `uv`, `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`, `ffplay`, and `deno` are shared Windows user installs. Read `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\references\ai-workspace\windows-cli-toolchain.md` for their paths, versions, usage, and PATH refresh steps. Before reinstalling, check `Get-Command`, the saved user PATH, and the WinGet package. A running IDE may have stale PATH; refresh the shell or restart that IDE. Keep Python dependencies in project environments.

<!-- HAND-OWNED since 2026-07-17. This file is the authority for Codex — edit it directly. On any major change, mirror it into CLAUDE.md so Claude and Codex stay in sync. -->
# Jay 2 — operating instructions for Codex (ai-os)

The canonical root is the repository directory containing this file (`C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` on this Windows machine). Resolve paths from the repository root instead of assuming a saved Mac or Windows absolute path. This is the router: it holds pointers, not knowledge. Read it, then follow the routing map to the right file instead of searching blind. Twin for Claude Code: `CLAUDE.md`.

## Identity and mission

You are **Jay 2**, Joshua's executive assistant. This root is his EA workspace and command center. Joshua is the founder of Aspiring Attorneys and builder of LSAT U (lsatprep.study), a premium LSAT prep platform; he is the product, voice, and content authority. Your job: take operations, admin, drafting, and research off his plate so his time goes to teaching and building. Frame work as decisions and tradeoffs, not basics.

Standing duties, run from here without re-briefing: student operations (transcripts, assignments, follow-ups via `students/`), prospect and consultation correspondence (drafted in his voice, **never auto-sent unless a pre-approved template**), platform actions on lsatprep.study, session-note ingestion from Gmail/Drive, and keeping the wiki ledgers (`tasks`/`hot`/`log`) current so the next session starts oriented.

## Routing map

Paths are relative to the repository root.

| Need | Path |
|---|---|
| **Who Joshua is** (identity, preferences, contact) | `context/about-me.md` |
| **The business** (offers, money, channels, gaps) | `context/work.md` |
| **Current priorities** (the 90-day slate) | `context/priorities.md` |
| **What we decided and why** (locked decisions) | `decisions/log.md` |
| Freshest session state (hot cache) | `wiki/hot.md` |
| LLM Wiki schema (source, page, ingest/query/lint rules) | `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` |
| Master index · work ledger · dated history | `wiki/index.md` · `wiki/tasks.md` · `wiki/log.md` |
| Product ground truth (pointers to live backends) | `wiki/dashboard.md` |
| LSAT teaching knowledge | `corpus/index.md` |
| A student — profile, transcripts, work, correspondence | `students/<name>/` — start at `students/README.md` |
| Voice for anything a student or prospect reads | `wiki/teaching-voice.md` |
| Was it sent? (every outbound draft/send) | `wiki/send-ledger.md` — append-only; Gmail is authority |
| Who the students are (name, email, sessions, links) | `students/roster.generated.md` — regenerate, never hand-edit |
| Learned facts and preferences (automemory) | native store `~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/` + `MEMORY.md` index |
| Skill / agent catalog | `skills/INDEX.md`, `agents/` |
| Systems Jay 2 can reach (registry) | `connections.md` — 7 data domains, mechanism/auth/freshness; read by `/audit` |
| Operator brain (how to think about automation) | `references/3ms-framework.md` — the Three Ms; walked weekly by `/level-up` |
| Automation build backlog (what to build next, ranked) | `references/automation-backlog.md` — corpus-derived gaps + tiers; feeds `/level-up` |
| How-it-works reference notes (methods, not state) | `references/` — also `references/claude-plus-codex.md`, `references/obsidian-command-center.md`, `references/second-brain-levels.md` |
| Web research and Firecrawl agents | `references/firecrawl.md` (guidelines in `references/firecrawl/`) |
| Shared Windows CLI tools for Codex, Claude Code, and Antigravity | `references/ai-workspace/windows-cli-toolchain.md` — installed paths, PATH refresh, and usage for `uv`, `yt-dlp`, FFmpeg, and Deno |
| Immutable source evidence · intake queue | `raw/sources/` · `raw/inbox/` — one inbox, one archive |
| Archived web clips, with provenance + hashes | `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` — clip + `PROVENANCE.md` + `SHA256SUMS` |
| Raw personal-data exports (Google Takeout, Gemini session transcripts) | `raw/data-about-me/` — unprocessed PII, git-ignored; never read wholesale |
| Design/plan thinking, dated (grill-me + interview outputs) | `brainstorms/` — supersedes the retired `aios/` reform folder |
| Session handoffs · chat exports | `handoffs/` — operational records, deliberately OUTSIDE `raw/`; `session-handoff` writes here |
| Current local work in Claude/Codex cloud | `setup/github-sync/README.md`; private `codex/local-sync/<machine>/<checkout>` branches and generated `.cloud-sync/` metadata/conversation exports. Cloud edits use separate task branches. |
| External teardowns and research, dated | `research/` |
| Web-clipped articles (Obsidian Web Clipper) | `raw/inbox/` — intake queue; `ingest` copies and hash-verifies clips into `raw/sources/clips/`; processed originals stay until removal is authorized, so inbox presence alone does not mean unprocessed |
| Application code | `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` — own repo, own instructions, own remote |
| Other projects (life-path, …) | `../ai-os-projects/<name>/` — each with its own `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/` |

Application repositories and dependencies stay outside this Obsidian vault, in sibling `../ai-os-projects/`. Do not create a junction from the vault back into that directory.

## Retrieval order

1. `wiki/hot.md` — current-session narrative and open threads.
2. `wiki/index.md` → the page (teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md`; a student: their folder). Follow links; there is no semantic search and none is needed at this size.
3. The live system — the final freshness escalation.

**Live systems are authority for operational state; the brain mirrors them:** Cal.com + Google Calendar own sessions scheduled/used, LSAT U Supabase owns homework status/dossier/publish state, Stripe owns payment events, Gmail owns correspondence. On any disagreement, the backend wins. The brain is primary — nothing upstream holds them — for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, product specs, and decisions.

**Do not read the wiki unless the task needs it.** Retrieval costs context every turn. Skip it for: code edits in `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/`; shell/Git/file ops; questions the injected memories already answer; a skill run that names its own inputs; anything mechanical and self-contained. Route to it for: what is X · what did we decide and why · who a student is and where they stand · anything drafted in Joshua's voice for someone else · any decision touching roadmap, pedagogy, or UX.

**LLM Wiki schema:** For knowledge intake, knowledge questions, durable synthesis, and wiki maintenance, follow `system/schema/llm-wiki.md`. Use `ingest` as the single intake entry point; keep sources immutable, compile into existing pages, update the complete fenced catalog in `wiki/index.md`, and append changes to `wiki/log.md`. Preserve domain homes and live-system authority. Mechanical tasks retain the retrieval exceptions above.

**Wiki operations (LLM-wiki pattern):** the LLM writes/maintains `wiki/` and `corpus/`; Joshua sources, explores, asks. Raw sources are immutable — read, never modify. *Ingest:* read the source from `raw/`, discuss takeaways, write/update the pages it touches, update `wiki/index.md`, append to `wiki/log.md` (`## [YYYY-MM-DD] ingest | Title`); one source at a time, supervised, unless Joshua says batch. *Query:* index first, then pages; file valuable answers back as pages so explorations compound. *Lint:* `second-brain-lint` for contradictions, stale claims, orphans, missing pages.

## Voice

- **No em-dashes, ever.** Joshua never types them; a stray em-dash reads as AI and costs trust.
- **No AI-slop vocabulary** — never "delve," "tapestry," "testament," "demystify," "boasts," "in the realm of," and their kin.
- **Anything a student or prospect will read** routes through `wiki/teaching-voice.md` and is drafted, never sent, unless it is a pre-approved template.
- **LSAT teaching:** use project-owned `lsat-u-voice` and `references/lsat-teaching-voice-standard.md`; this Joshua-selected standard takes precedence over older vendor voice blends.
- **When an outbound message is authorized**, preserve the draft's paragraph breaks, list lines, and intentional spacing. After sending, compare the recipient-facing rendering with the draft and confirm exactly one copy landed. Report any mismatch; never resend automatically.
- **Lead with your recommendation, not a menu.** Be decisive; don't over-hedge.
- **Any prompt Joshua will paste elsewhere** goes inside a fenced code block, never inline prose.

## Tools, keys, and secrets

- **The entire secret model is `.env` + `.gitignore`.** The root `.env` (git-ignored — verify with `git check-ignore .env`) is Joshua's single central store for **everything**: API keys, passwords, and bank/account info (decided 2026-07-17). OAuth client secrets live in `~/.config/gws/`, outside the repo. There is no vault, no Keychain, no encrypted store (`records.sparsebundle` is dead) and none is wanted. Never paste a credential into chat — add it to `.env` directly in the editor.
- **Safety is the tool layer, not prompts:** allow/deny tool lists + scoped API keys. Jay 2 acts under its own credentials and never impersonates Joshua.
- **Connected live systems:** Cal.com, Google Calendar, LSAT U Supabase, Stripe, Gmail. Full registry in `connections.md`.
- **Google access is `gws` CLI only** (`gws gmail|drive|calendar|docs …`, keyring-auth; see `skills/codex/gws-shared/`). Never the Google connector MCPs — the CLI is faster, batchable, and exports Docs to markdown.
- **Shared Windows command tools:** use `uv`, `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`, `ffplay`, and `deno` from the Windows user PATH. For versions, locations, how to invoke them, or a stale terminal environment, read `references/ai-workspace/windows-cli-toolchain.md`. Check before reinstalling; restart the affected IDE when its inherited PATH is stale.
- **Government IDs stay outside this OS.** Everything else (student names, emails, schedules, transcripts, notes, payment *links*) is ordinary local working material — classify by content, never by filename.
- **Git remote is enabled.** `origin` is GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/ai-os`, decided 2026-09-29). The outer repo never tracks `.env`, `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` (own repo), runtime logs, or locks. Do not create gitlinks.
- **New laptop restore:** GitHub contains tracked ai-os files and skills. It deliberately excludes `.env`, `raw/`, app repos, credentials, and machine sessions. Restore machine-specific files from the private backup using `setup/backup/README.md` and its `README - START HERE.md`; install tools separately and sign in again where sessions cannot be restored. Never move credentials, raw personal data, or active browser tokens into GitHub.
- **Authorized cloud transfer (2026-10-07):** private sync branches also carry portable skills, memory and conversation exports. Joshua explicitly authorized credential transfer; implementation uses the authenticated encrypted package described in `setup/github-sync/README.md`; plaintext secrets and its unlock key stay outside GitHub and Drive. Cloud authentication and native browser sessions still require separate verification.

## Skills

Everything is a skill — **there are no slash commands** (decided 2026-07-17: one kind, one home, one activation path). The catalog at `skills/` and `agents/` is inert storage; nothing auto-loads. An item runs only once **activated by copy** (not symlink) into `.agents/skills/` (Claude: `.claude/skills/`). Creating one writes to **both** the project and the catalog (`skills/{claude,codex}/`), project-scoped — never auto-global. `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees are never merged; a vendor item is a live symlink + a frozen `_snapshots/` copy. `system/scripts/check-skills.sh` validates the model; `skills/INDEX.md` indexes it.

Active in this root. Run `pickup` only when Joshua explicitly asks for it. Do not run it automatically at session start. Use `wrapup` at the end of substantive sessions:

| Skill | Use it when |
|---|---|
| `pickup` / `wrapup` | `pickup` only on Joshua's explicit request / `wrapup` at session end (wrapup persists to the wiki ledgers) |
| `session-handoff` | mid-work context reset near ~250k, or passing work Codex↔Claude — chat-only, writes nothing |
| `audit` | scoring the OS against the Four Cs (structural health, /100) |
| `level-up` | weekly 3Ms interview to find + ship one automation |
| `consultation-inquiry-drafts`, `-followup-drafts`, `inbound-tutoring-replies` | drafting to prospects/students — always via the teaching voice |
| `transcript-intake` | retrieving, filing or reconciling student/session transcripts; separate from knowledge `ingest` |
| `guided-drill-assignment` | assigning a student a drill |
| `lsat-u-platform-actions` | doing anything ON lsatprep.study from here (assign, post, message, ingest) |
| `tutorocean-profile-fill` | TutorOcean profile work |
| `prompt-architect-refiner` | refining a prompt before it ships |
| `accommodations-email` | "accommodations email to X" (dictation: "accommodations scale"); pre-approved send, QP form attached, self-logging |

When a skill run exposes a gap, improve the skill before moving on. Runtime-specific catalog differences must be justified in `skills/PARITY.md`; undeclared differences are defects.

## Guardrails

- **95% rule.** Make no changes until you are 95% confident what Joshua wants. Ask until you are.
- **Lovable credits require confirmation.** Never take an action that spends or may spend Lovable credits without Joshua's explicit confirmation immediately before the action. Do not infer approval from a broader request to fix, build, deploy, or continue. Free Lovable controls and direct deployment paths may be used without this credit-spend confirmation.
- **Lovable Cloud deploys through Lovable.** LSAT U currently uses a Lovable Cloud-managed backend. Deploy its Edge Functions through the Lovable agent, not the Supabase CLI, unless the backend has first been migrated to a separately managed Supabase project. Confirm this route with Joshua before starting a Lovable deployment.
- **Root stays thin.** Don't accumulate files at the root; work products go to their homes (wiki, students, corpus, projects). Anything left at root gets filed before the session ends.
- **Delegate bulk work.** Exploration or analysis spanning 3+ files goes to a sub-agent that returns summaries, not file dumps.
- **Shell ergonomics.** Run one command per call — chained `cmd; cmd` and `rm -rf X && clone Y` forms trip permission denials that the split commands would not. Never poll with `sleep`. Use backgrounded execution for anything slow, and raise the timeout explicitly for known-slow ops (large moves, typecheck-gated pushes, full verify runs) rather than eating the default.
- **Search before creating.** One authoritative page per subject — update it, don't grow a parallel truth. `wiki/index.md` catalogs every page and updates on every ingest.
- **Preserve provenance and source hashes.** Never move, delete, or modify a migration source root — copy and hash-verify. Never follow a source symlink outside its registered root.
- **Conflicts stay explicit until adjudicated** — never silently overwrite one source generation with another.
- **Automation writes only generated files or fenced generated sections,** through compare-and-swap.
- **Obey nested instruction files** — they win over broader ones for their subtree. LSATU's recovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` are inviolate: never regenerate them without Joshua's explicit approval.
- **Memory = native automemory.** "Remember that…" writes one fact per file to the native store; `MEMORY.md` indexes it. This file is the rules; memory is learned facts — never mix them. Transcripts and raw captures never auto-promote.
- **Append meaningful system changes** to `wiki/log.md`; log locked decisions to `decisions/log.md`.

## Dual-runtime (Codex + Claude)

- Begin at the Git-root `AGENTS.md` and follow directory-scoped instruction files toward the working path; the nearest file wins for its subtree.
- Keep root instructions inside the runtime byte budget. Move narrow rules to the narrowest owning directory rather than growing this file.
- Activated skills are **copies** in `.agents/skills/` (from `skills/codex/`); agent definitions convert to `.codex/agents/*.toml` with the instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- Claude reads `CLAUDE.md` and never this file. Anything that must hold for both runtimes goes in BOTH files — when you change it here, mirror it there; keep the routing map and guardrails byte-identical between them.
- Activation is unequal by design: Codex reads copies in `.agents/skills/`, Claude reads copies in `.claude/skills/`. Codex and Claude both activate the LLM Wiki ingest, query, and lint workflows. Other runtime-specific activation differences remain declared in `skills/PARITY.md`. Never have separate runtimes writing the same page concurrently.
- Never have Claude and Codex editing the same file at once; pass work between them by session handoff (summarize → clear → paste). At ~250k tokens, hand off rather than riding autocompact.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

Found the exact Claude chat: **“Spreeder website analysis.”**

The goal is to build **Reading Center** to match Spreeder’s screens and reading behavior, then integrate it into LSAT U.

- **Built:** five reading modes, library management, tags, bookmarks, preferences, speed controls, search, Profile, stats, and goals.
- **Still needs verification:** import, fullscreen, finish alert, themes, shortcuts, visible Flow playback, and remaining Profile behavior against Spreeder.
- **Still needs implementation:** LSAT U integration and its database migration. The LSAT U copy is older than the standalone app.
- **Excluded by prior decision:** AI generation and summaries. Import currently supports text, Markdown, and HTML.

I restarted the app, opened [Reading Center](http://127.0.0.1:8100), and verified **30 passing tests, a clean TypeScript check, and a successful build**. Profile’s main screens also opened correctly.

I recorded the takeover in the [existing spec](/C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/research/2026-10-13-spreeder-reconstruction-spec.md). The next step is completing standalone browser verification before porting it into LSAT U.

\\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { createPortal } from \\"react-dom\\";\\r\\nimport { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from \\"react-router-dom\\";\\r\\nimport { useDeletePassage, usePassages, useProgressList, useSaveProgress, useUpsertPassage } from \\"../hooks/useReadingCenterData\\";\\r\\nimport { buildTextModel } from \\"../lib/spreeder\\";\\r\\nimport { rcReaderPath, RC_LIBRARY_PATH } from \\"../lib/routes\\";\\r\\nimport type { Passage, Progress } from \\"../lib/types\\";\\r\\nimport { Icon, MIcon, type IconName } from \\"../components/Icon\\";\\r\\nimport { TopBar } from \\"../components/AppShell\\";\\r\\nimport { AddTextScreen } from \\"../components/AddText\\";\\r\\nimport { ViewPanel } from \\"../components/ViewPanel\\";\\r\\nimport { Modal, DialogHeader } from \\"../components/spreeder-ui\\";\\r\\nimport { ConfirmDialog, TagTextDialog } from \\"../components/Tags\\";\\r\\nimport { NothingHereYet } from \\"./TagsPage\\";\\r\\n\\r\\ntype ShelfKey = \\"recent\\" | \\"added\\" | \\"progress\\" | \\"finished\\";\\r\\nconst SHELF_TITLES: Record<ShelfKey, string> = { recent: \\"Recent\\", added: \\"Added By Me\\", progress: \\"In Progress\\", finished: \\"Finished\\" };\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function LibraryPage(): React.ReactElement {\\r\\n  const { tag } = useParams<{ tag?: string }>();\\r\\n  const passages = usePassages().data ?? [];\\r\\n  const progress = useProgressList().data ?? [];\\r\\n  const saveProgress = useSaveProgress();\\r\\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState(\\"\\");\\r\\n  const [viewAll, setViewAll] = useState<ShelfKey | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [menu, setMenu] = useState<{ passage: Passage; anchor: DOMRect; shelf: ShelfKey } | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [action, setAction] = useState<{ kind: \\"view\\" | \\"image\\" | \\"edit\\" | \\"tag\\" | \\"delete\\"; passage: Passage } | null>(null);\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n\\r\\n  const prog = useMemo(() => new Map<string, Progress>(progress.map((p) => [p.passage_id, p])), [progress]);\\r\\n  const visible = passages.filter((p) => p.is_published && (!tag || (p.tags ?? []).includes(tag)));\\r\\n\\r\\n  const shelves: Record<ShelfKey, Passage[]> = useMemo(() => {\\r\\n    const recent = visible\\r\\n      .filter((p) => prog.has(p.id) && !prog.get(p.id)!.hidden_from_recent)\\r\\n      .sort((a, b) => prog.get(b.id)!.last_opened_at.localeCompare(prog.get(a.id)!.last_opened_at));\\r\\n    const added = [...visible].sort((a, b) => b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at));\\r\\n    const inProgress = visible.filter((p) => {\\r\\n      const pr = prog.get(p.id);\\r\\n      return !!pr && pr.word_index > 0 && !pr.completed_at;\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    const finished = visible.filter((p) => !!prog.get(p.id)?.completed_at);\\r\\n    return { recent, added, progress: inProgress, finished };\\r\\n  }, [visible, prog]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();\\r\\n  const results = needle ? visible.filter((p) => p.title.toLowerCase().includes(needle) || (p.author ?? \\"\\").toLowerCase().includes(needle)) : null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const card = (p: Passage, shelf: ShelfKey) => (\\r\\n    <PassageCard key={`${shelf}:${p.id}`} passage={p} onMenu={(anchor) => setMenu({ passage: p, anchor, shelf })} />\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"min-h-screen\\">\\r\\n      <TopBar query={query} onQuery={setQuery} placeholder={`Searching ${visible.length} ${visible.length === 1 ? \\"text\\" : \\"texts\\"}..`} />\\r\\n      <div className=\\"px-5 pb-10\\">\\r\\n        {tag && !results ? (\\r\\n          visible.length === 0 ? <NothingHereYet /> : <Shelf title={tag} wrap>{visible.map((p) => card(p, \\"added\\"))}</Shelf>\\r\\n        ) : results ? (\\r\\n          <Shelf title={`Results (${results.length})`}>{results.map((p) => card(p, \\"added\\"))}</Shelf>\\r\\n        ) : viewAll ? (\\r\\n          <Shelf title={SHELF_TITLES[viewAll]} back={() => setViewAll(null)} wrap>{shelves[viewAll].map((p) => card(p, viewAll))}</Shelf>\\r\\n        ) : visible.length === 0 ? (\\r\\n          <p className=\\"mt-16 text-center text-base\\" style={{ color: \\"var(--rc-text-2)\\" }}>{tag ? \\"No texts with this tag.\\" : \\"Your library is empty. Use Add Text to add something to read.\\"}</p>\\r\\n        ) : (\\r\\n          ([\\"recent\\", \\"added\\", \\"progress\\", \\"finished\\"] as ShelfKey[]).map((k) =>\\r\\n            shelves[k].length === 0 ? null : (\\r\\n              <Shelf key={k} title={SHELF_TITLES[k]} onViewAll={k === \\"recent\\" ? undefined : () => setViewAll(k)}>\\r\\n                {shelves[k].map((p) => card(p, k))}\\r\\n              </Shelf>\\r\\n            ),\\r\\n          )\\r\\n        )}\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      {menu && (\\r\\n        <CardMenu\\r\\n          anchor={menu.anchor}\\r\\n          showHide={menu.shelf === \\"recent\\"}\\r\\n          onClose={() => setMenu(null)}\\r\\n          onPick={(kind) => {\\r\\n            const p = menu.passage;\\r\\n            setMenu(null);\\r\\n            if (kind === \\"hide\\") {\\r\\n              const pr = prog.get(p.id);\\r\\n              saveProgress.mutate({ passage_id: p.id, word_index: pr?.word_index ?? text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"@'\nimport { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { toast } from \"sonner\";\nimport { useAuth } from \"@/contexts/AuthContext\";\nimport { supabase } from \"@/integrations/supabase/client\";\nimport { rcDb } from \"../lib/db\";\nimport { DEFAULT_PREFERENCES } from \"../lib/constants\";\nimport { countWords, slugify } from \"../lib/tokenize\";\nimport { mergePassageMetadata, metadataForEdit, progressPayload, readingCenterKey, type PassageMetadata } from \"../lib/persistence\";\nimport type { Passage, Preferences, Progress, Bookmark, ReadingSession, UserTag } from \"../lib/types\";\n\nasync function checked<T>(request: PromiseLike<{ data?: unknown; error: { message: string } | null }>): Promise<T> {\n  const { data, error } = await request;\n  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);\n  return data as T;\n}\nfunction requireUser(userId?: string) {\n  if (!userId) throw new Error(\"Sign in to save your reading work.\");\n  return userId;\n}\nconst report = (error: Error) => toast.error(`Reading Center: ${error.message}`);\n\nasync function passagesForUser(userId: string, raw = false): Promise<Passage[]> {\n  const rows = await checked<Passage[]>(rcDb(\"reading_center_passages\").select(\"*\").order(\"sort_order\").order(\"title\"));\n  if (raw) return rows ?? [];\n  const metadata = await checked<PassageMetadata[]>(rcDb(\"reading_center_passage_metadata\").select(\"*\").eq(\"user_id\", userId));\n  const byId = new Map((metadata ?? []).map((row) => [row.passage_id, row]));\n  return (rows ?? []).map((row) => mergePassageMetadata(row, byId.get(row.id))).filter((row): row is Passage => !!row);\n}\n\nexport function usePassages(raw = false) {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  return useQuery({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"passages\", raw), queryFn: () => passagesForUser(requireUser(user?.id), raw), enabled: !!user });\n}\nexport function usePassageBySlug(slug: string | undefined) {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"passage\", slug), enabled: !!user && !!slug,\n    queryFn: async () => {\n      const row = await checked<Passage | null>(rcDb(\"reading_center_passages\").select(\"*\").eq(\"slug\", slug).maybeSingle());\n      if (!row) return null;\n      const metadata = await checked<PassageMetadata | null>(rcDb(\"reading_center_passage_metadata\").select(\"*\").eq(\"user_id\", requireUser(user?.id)).eq(\"passage_id\", row.id).maybeSingle());\n      return mergePassageMetadata(row, metadata ?? undefined);\n    },\n  });\n}\nexport function usePreferences() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  return useQuery({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"prefs\"), enabled: !!user, queryFn: async (): Promise<Preferences> => {\n    const row = await checked<Partial<Preferences> | null>(rcDb(\"reading_center_preferences\").select(\"*\").eq(\"user_id\", requireUser(user?.id)).maybeSingle());\n    return { ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, ...row, line_spacing: Number(row?.line_spacing ?? DEFAULT_PREFERENCES.line_spacing) };\n  } });\n}\nexport function useSavePreferences() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  const client = useQueryClient();\n  return useMutation({ mutationFn: async (input: Partial<Preferences>) => {\n    const userId = requireUser(user?.id);\n    // Only changed columns are written so rapid independent controls cannot overwrite one another.\n    await checked(rcDb(\"reading_center_preferences\").upsert({ user_id: userId, ...input, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, { onConflict: \"user_id\" }));\n    return input;\n  }, onMutate: async (input) => {\n    const key = readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"prefs\");\n    await client.cancelQueries({ queryKey: key });\n    const previous = client.getQueryData<Preferences>(key);\n    client.setQueryData(key, { ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, ...previous, ...input });\n    return { previous };\n  }, onError: (error, _input, context) => {\n    if (context?.previous) client.setQueryData(readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"prefs\"), context.previous);\n    report(error);\n  }, onSettled: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"prefs\") }) });\n}\nexport function useProgressList() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  return useQuery({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"progress\"), enabled: !!user, queryFn: () => checked<Progress[]>(rcDb(\"reading_center_progress\").select(\"*\").eq(\"user_id\", requireUser(user?.id))) });\n}\nexport interface SaveProgressInput { passage_id: string; word_index: number; completed?: boolean; hidden_from_recent?: boolean }\nexport function useSaveProgress() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  const client = useQueryClient();\n  return useMutation({ mutationFn: async (input: SaveProgressInput) => {\n    const userId = requireUser(user?.id);\n    // Only update explicit fields: opening at zero preserves completion; restart explicitly clears it.\n    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = { user_id: userId, passage_id: input.passage_id, word_index: Math.max(0, Math.floor(input.word_index)), last_opened_at: new Date().toISOString() };\n    if (input.completed !== undefined) payload.completed_at = input.completed ? new Date().toISOString() : null;\n    if (input.hidden_from_recent !== undefined) payload.hidden_from_recent = input.hidden_from_recent;\n    await checked(rcDb(\"reading_center_progress\").upsert(payload, { onConflict: \"user_id,passage_id\" }));\n  }, onError: report, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"progress\") }) });\n}\nexport function useBookmarks(passageId?: string) {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  return useQuery({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"bookmarks\", passageId), enabled: !!user && !!passageId, queryFn: () => checked<Bookmark[]>(rcDb(\"reading_center_bookmarks\").select(\"*\").eq(\"user_id\", requireUser(user?.id)).eq(\"passage_id\", passageId).order(\"created_at\", { ascending: false })) });\n}\nfunction useScopedMutation<T>(mutationFn: (input: T, userId: string) => Promise<unknown>, scope?: string) {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  const client = useQueryClient();\n  return useMutation({ mutationFn: (input: T) => mutationFn(input, requireUser(user?.id)), onError: report, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, ...(scope ? [scope] : [])) }) });\n}\nexport function useAddBookmark() {\n  return useScopedMutation((input: { passage_id: string; word_index: number; note?: string | null }, userId) => checked<Bookmark>(rcDb(\"reading_center_bookmarks\").insert({ ...input, user_id: userId }).select().single()), \"bookmarks\");\n}\nexport function useUpdateBookmark() {\n  return useScopedMutation((input: { id: string; note: string | null }, userId) => checked(rcDb(\"reading_center_bookmarks\").update({ note: input.note }).eq(\"id\", input.id).eq(\"user_id\", userId)), \"bookmarks\");\n}\nexport function useDeleteBookmark() {\n  return useScopedMutation((input: { id: string; passage_id?: string }, userId) => checked(rcDb(\"reading_center_bookmarks\").delete().eq(\"id\", input.id).eq(\"user_id\", userId)), \"bookmarks\");\n}\nexport function useSessions(sinceIso?: string) {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  return useQuery({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"sessions\", sinceIso), enabled: !!user, queryFn: () => {\n    let query = rcDb(\"reading_center_sessions\").select(\"*\").eq(\"user_id\", requireUser(user?.id));\n    if (sinceIso) query = query.gte(\"started_at\", sinceIso);\n    return checked<ReadingSession[]>(query.order(\"started_at\", { ascending: true }));\n  } });\n}\nexport function useRecordSession() {\n  return useScopedMutation((session: Omit<ReadingSession, \"id\">, userId) => checked(rcDb(\"reading_center_sessions\").insert({ ...session, user_id: userId })), \"sessions\");\n}\n\nexport function useUpsertPassage() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  const client = useQueryClient();\n  return useMutation({ mutationFn: async (input: Partial<Passage> & { title: string; body: string }): Promise<Passage> => {\n    const userId = requireUser(user?.id);\n    const existing = input.id ? await checked<Passage>(rcDb(\"reading_center_passages\").select(\"*\").eq(\"id\", input.id).single()) : null;\n    // The teacher manager passes owner_id:null explicitly to edit original shared texts.\n    const manageShared = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(input, \"owner_id\") && input.owner_id === null;\n    let row = existing;\n    if (!existing || existing.owner_id === userId || manageShared) {\n      const { tags, ...fields } = input;\n      row = await checked<Passage>(rcDb(\"reading_center_passages\").upsert({\n        ...fields,\n        owner_id: existing?.owner_id ?? (manageShared ? null : userId),\n        slug: existing?.slug ?? input.slug ?? `${slugify(input.title)}-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`,\n        word_count: countWords(input.body), updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n      }).select().single());\n    }\n    const metadata = existing && !manageShared && existing.owner_id !== userId ? metadataForEdit(input) : input.tags ? { tags: input.tags } : null;\n    if (metadata) await checked(rcDb(\"reading_center_passage_metadata\").upsert({ ...metadata, user_id: userId, passage_id: row!.id }, { onConflict: \"user_id,passage_id\" }));\n    return mergePassageMetadata(row!, metadata ?? undefined)!;\n  }, onError: report, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id) }) });\n}\nexport function useDeletePassage() {\n  return useScopedMutation(async (id: string, userId) => {\n    const row = await checked<Passage>(rcDb(\"reading_center_passages\").select(\"*\").eq(\"id\", id).single());\n    if (row.owner_id === userId) await checked(rcDb(\"reading_center_passages\").delete().eq(\"id\", id).eq(\"owner_id\", userId));\n    else await checked(rcDb(\"reading_center_passage_metadata\").upsert({ user_id: userId, passage_id: id, hidden: true }, { onConflict: \"user_id,passage_id\" }));\n  });\n}\nexport function useReorderPassages() {\n  return useScopedMutation(async (items: { id: string; sort_order: number }[]) => {\n    for (const item of items) await checked(rcDb(\"reading_center_passages\").update({ sort_order: item.sort_order }).eq(\"id\", item.id));\n  });\n}\nexport function useTags() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  return useQuery({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"tags\"), enabled: !!user, queryFn: () => checked<UserTag[]>(rcDb(\"reading_center_tags\").select(\"name,color\").eq(\"user_id\", requireUser(user?.id)).order(\"created_at\")) });\n}\nexport function useSaveTag() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  const client = useQueryClient();\n  return useMutation({ mutationFn: async ({ oldName, name, color }: { oldName?: string; name: string; color?: number }): Promise<UserTag> => {\n    const userId = requireUser(user?.id);\n    const clean = name.trim();\n    if (!clean) throw new Error(\"Enter a tag name.\");\n    const existing = client.getQueryData<UserTag[]>(readingCenterKey(userId, \"tags\"))?.find((tag) => tag.name === oldName);\n    const saved = { name: clean, color: color ?? existing?.color ?? 0 };\n    if (oldName) await checked((supabase as any).rpc(\"reading_center_rename_tag\", { p_old_name: oldName, p_new_name: clean, p_color: saved.color }));\n    else await checked(rcDb(\"reading_center_tags\").insert({ ...saved, user_id: userId }));\n    return saved;\n  }, onError: report, onSuccess: () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id) }) });\n}\nexport function useDeleteTag() {\n  return useScopedMutation((name: string) => checked((supabase as any).rpc(\"reading_center_delete_tag\", { p_name: name })));\n}\nexport function useReadingProfile() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  return useQuery({ queryKey: readingCenterKey(user?.id, \"profile\"), enabled: !!user, queryFn: async () => {\n    const row = await checked<{ display_name: string } | null>(rcDb(\"reading_center_profile\").select(\"display_name\").eq(\"user_id\", requireUser(user?.id)).maybeSingle());\n    return row?.display_name || user?.user_metadata?.full_name || \"Reader\";\n  } });\n}\nexport function useSaveReadingProfile() {\n  return useScopedMutation((name: string, userId) => checked(rcDb(\"reading_center_profile\").upsert({ user_id: userId, display_name: name.trim() || \"Reader\" }, { onConflict: \"user_id\" })), \"profile\");\n}\n'@ | Set-Content -LiteralPath src/features/reading-center/hooks/useReadingCenterData.ts",workdir:"C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center",max_output_tokens:1000}));

(os error 2)\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"bb6142","wall_time_seconds":0.4547043,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":596,"output":"import React from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { TopBar } from \\"../components/AppShell\\";\\r\\nimport { Icon } from \\"../components/Icon\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Courses: Spreeder shows this empty state when no class courses are assigned. */\\r\\nexport default function CoursesPage(): React.ReactElement {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"min-h-screen\\">\\r\\n      <TopBar />\\r\\n      <div className=\\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-5 pt-32 text-center\\">\\r\\n        <Icon name=\\"school\\" size={84} style={{ color: \\"var(--rc-text-2)\\" }} />\\r\\n        <p className=\\"mt-6 text-lg\\" style={{ color: \\"var(--rc-text)\\" }}>No curriculums or courses assigned to your class yet.</p>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n/** One reading visit: completed chunks contribute words; pauses contribute focus time. */\\r\\nexport class ReadingVisit {\\r\\n  private startedAt: number | null = null;\\r\\n  private activeSince: number | null = null;\\r\\n  private pausedSince: number | null = null;\\r\\n  private activeMs = 0;\\r\\n  private pausedMs = 0;\\r\\n  private wordsRead = 0;\\r\\n\\r\\n  play(now = Date.now()): void {\\r\\n    if (this.activeSince != null) return;\\r\\n    if (this.startedAt == null) this.startedAt = now;\\r\\n    if (this.pausedSince != null) this.pausedMs += Math.max(0, now - this.pausedSince);\\r\\n    this.pausedSince = null;\\r\\n    this.activeSince = now;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  pause(now = Date.now()): void {\\r\\n    if (this.activeSince == null) return;\\r\\n    this.activeMs += Math.max(0, now - this.activeSince);\\r\\n    this.activeSince = null;\\r\\n    this.pausedSince = now;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  read(words: number): void {\\r\\n    this.wordsRead += Math.max(0, words);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  finish(now = Date.now()): { words_read: number; active_ms: number; paused_ms: number; started_at: string; ended_at: string } | null {\\r\\n    const activeMs = this.activeMs + (this.activeSince != null ? Math.max(0, now - this.activeSince) : 0);\\r\\n    const pausedMs = this.pausedMs + (this.pausedSince != null ? Math.max(0, now - this.pausedSince) : 0);\\r\\n    const result = this.startedAt != null && activeMs >= 5000 && this.wordsRead > 0 ? {\\r\\n      words_read: this.wordsRead,\\r\\n      active_ms: activeMs,\\r\\n      paused_ms: pausedMs,\\r\\n      started_at: new Date(this.startedAt).toISOString(),\\r\\n      ended_at: new Date(now).toISOString(),\\r\\n    } : null;\\r\\n    this.startedAt = this.activeSince = this.pausedSince = null;\\r\\n    this.activeMs = this.pausedMs = this.wordsRead = 0;\\r\\n    return result;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"06fdaa","wall_time_seconds":0.254553,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}]

} from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { toast } from \'sonner\';\\r\\nimport { z } from \'zod\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst signUpSchema = z.object({\\r\\n  email: z.string().email(\'Invalid email address\').max(255, \'Email too long\'),\\r\\n  password: [REDACTED]    .min(8, \'Password must be at least 8 characters\')\\r\\n    .max(100, \'Password too long\'),\\r\\n  name: z.string()\\r\\n    .min(1, \'Name is required\')\\r\\n    .max(50, \'Name must be at most 50 characters\')\\r\\n});\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface AuthContextValue {\\r\\n  user: User | null;\\r\\n  session: Session | null;\\r\\n  loading: boolean;\\r\\n  /** True once the auth state has been definitively resolved (safe for route guards) */\\r\\n  authReady: boolean;\\r\\n  signUp: (email: string, password: [REDACTED], name: string) => Promise<{ error: any }>;\\r\\n  signIn: (email: string, password: [REDACTED] => Promise<{ error: any }>;\\r\\n  signOut: (opts?: { keepSavedEmail?: boolean }) => Promise<{ error: any }>;\\r\\n  resetPassword: (email: string) => Promise<{ error: any }>;\\r\\n  updatePassword: (newPassword: string) => Promise<{ error: any }>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Detect if the current page load is mid-OAuth callback (URL-based only) */\\r\\nfunction isOAuthCallbackUrl(): boolean {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    window.location.hash.includes(\'access_token\') ||\\r\\n    window.location.search.includes(\'code=\')\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\r\\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\\r\\n  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\\r\\n  const [authReady, setAuthReady] = useState(false);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Track whether we detected an OAuth callback at mount time\\r\\n  const isOAuthRef = useRef(isOAuthCallbackUrl());\\r\\n  // Safety timeout so we never stay loading forever\\r\\n  const oauthTimeoutRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);\\r\\n\\r\\n  /** Every route guard now blocks on `authReady`, so it must always be reached. */\\r\\n  const AUTH_RESOLVE_CEILING_MS = 15000;\\r\\n\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    // Ensure a students row exists for this auth user so class_id is always resolvable\\r\\n    const provisionStudentRecord = async (userId: string) => {\\r\\n      const { data: existing, error: readError } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'students\')\\r\\n        .select(\'class_id\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'user_id\', userId)\\r\\n        .maybeSingle();\\r\\n\\r\\n      // A swallowed read error yields existing=null and wrongly attempts a\\r\\n      // duplicate insert; a swallowed insert error leaves the user with no\\r\\n      // students row (class_id never resolvable). Surface both.\\r\\n      if (readError) {\\r\\n        console.error(\'[auth] provision read failed:\', readError.message);\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (!existing) {\\r\\n        const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from(\'students\').insert({\\r\\n          user_id: userId,\\r\\n          class_id: userId,\\r\\n          token_hash: userId,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        // Ignore a unique-violation race (a parallel provision won); log the rest.\\r\\n        if (insertError && insertError.code !== \'23505\') {\\r\\n          console.error(\'[auth] provision insert failed:\', insertError.message);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    };\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Guards against a resolution arriving after teardown (a StrictMode\\r\\n    // remount, or a slow getSession landing on an unmounted provider). Without\\r\\n    // it a late resolve writes state into a dead tree and, worse, can flip\\r\\n    // authReady back and forth while route guards are reading it.\\r\\n    let cancelled = false;\\r\\n\\r\\n    const markReady = (s: Session | null) => {\\r\\n      if (cancelled) return;\\r\\n      setSession(s);\\r\\n      // Keep the user object\'s identity stable across TOKEN_REFRESHED. Supabase\\r\\n      // fires that roughly hourly and on tab refocus with a fresh object for the\\r\\n      // same person; handing it straight to setUser re-runs every effect keyed on\\r\\n      // `user` downstream. One of those rebuilt a live drill session mid-lesson.\\r\\n      setUser((prev) => {\\r\\n        const next = s?.user ?? null;\\r\\n        if (prev && next && prev.id === next.id) return prev;\\r\\n        return next;\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      setLoading(false);\\r\\n      setAuthReady(true);\\r\\n      sessionStorage.removeItem(\'oauth_pending\');\\r\\n      if (oauthTimeoutRef.current) {\\r\\n        clearTimeout(oauthTimeoutRef.current);\\r\\n        oauthTimeoutRef.current = null;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (s?.user) {\\r\\n        provisionStudentRecord(s.user.id);\\r\\n        initCloudMirror(s.user.id);\\r\\n      } else {\\r\\n        stopCloudMirror();\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    };\\r\\n\\r\\n    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(\\r\\n      (event, newSession) => {\\r\\n        if (event === \'PASSWORD_RECOVERY\') {\\r\\n          markReady(newSession);\\r\\n          window.location.replace(\'/reset-password\');\\r\\n          return;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        if (newSession) {\\r\\n          markReady(newSession);\\r\\n        } else if (!isOAuthRef.current) {\\r\\n          markReady(null);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    );\\r\\n\\r\\n    // THEN check for existing session\\r\\n    supabase.auth.getSession().then(\\r\\n      ({ data: { session: existingSession } }) => {\\r\\n        if (existingSession) {\\r\\n          markReady(existingSession);\\r\\n        } else if (!isOAuthRef.current) {\\r\\n          markReady(null);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      },\\r\\n      (err) => {\\r\\n        // A rejected getSession used to leave authReady false forever. Now that\\r\\n        // every ProtectedRoute blocks on authReady, that would be a permanently\\r\\n        // spinning app rather than a degraded one — so resolve to signed-out and\\r\\n        // let the guards do their job.\\r\\n        console.error(\'[auth] getSession failed:\', err);\\r\\n        if (!isOAuthRef.current) markReady(null);\\r\\n      },\\r\\n    );\\r\\n\\r\\n    // Silent safety net: Auth.tsx owns the user-visible 10s return-leg timeout.\\r\\n    // This timer is longer (15s) so it only fires if the page is in an unusual\\r\\n    // state where Auth.tsx\'s timeout never ran. No toast, no user-visible signal —\\r\\n    // just unfreeze the UI so route guards can act on `user = null`.\\r\\n    // Armed on every load, not only the OAuth leg. Route guards block on\\r\\n    // authReady, so any path that never resolves — a hung network, an SDK that\\r\\n    // neither resolves nor rejects — would otherwise spin forever.\\r\\n"}'}]

initialPrefs: Preferences; saved: Progress | undefined }): React.ReactElement {\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const savePrefs = useSavePreferences();\\r\\n  const saveProgress = useSaveProgress();\\r\\n  const recordSession = useRecordSession();\\r\\n  const bookmarks = useBookmarks(passage.id);\\r\\n  const addBookmark = useAddBookmark();\\r\\n  const updateBookmark = useUpdateBookmark();\\r\\n  const deleteBookmark = useDeleteBookmark();\\r\\n\\r\\n  const model = useMemo(() => buildTextModel(passage.body), [passage.body]);\\r\\n  const [prefs, setPrefs] = useState<Preferences>({ ...initialPrefs, wpm: clampWpm(initialPrefs.wpm), font_size: clampFontSize(initialPrefs.font_size) });\\r\\n  const updatePrefs = useCallback(\\r\\n    (p: Partial<Preferences>) => {\\r\\n      setPrefs((cur) => ({ ...cur, ...p }));\\r\\n      savePrefs.mutate(p);\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    [savePrefs],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  const fontStack = FONTS.find((f) => f.label === prefs.font_family)?.stack ?? Math.min(saved.word_index, model.words.length - 1) : 0;\\r\\n  const engine = useSpreederEngine({ model, prefs, stage, measure, initialWord: startWord, onFinish: complete, onReadChunk: (words) => visit.current.read(words) });\\r\\n  const { activeWord, playing } = engine;\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Spreeder pauses and relays out on resize.\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (visitMeta.current.reading_mode !== prefs.reading_mode) {\\r\\n      flushSession();\\r\\n      visitMeta.current = { reading_mode: prefs.reading_mode, wpm: prefs.wpm };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }, [prefs.reading_mode, prefs.wpm, flushSession]);\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const onResize = () => engine.pause();\\r\\n    window.addEventListener(\\"resize\\", onResize);\\r\\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\\"resize\\", onResize);\\r\\n  }, [engine.pause]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Opening a text puts it back on the Recent shelf.\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    saveProgress.mutate({ passage_id: passage.id, word_index: startWord, hidden_from_recent: false });\\r\\n  }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\\r\\n\\r\\n  // ---- reading sessions (feed the Profile stats)\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (playing) visit.current.play();\\r\\n    else visit.current.pause();\\r\\n  }, [playing]);\\r\\n  useEffect(() => () => flushSession(), [flushSession]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // ---- dialogs\\r\\n  const [prefsOpen, setPrefsOpen] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const [wpmOpen, setWpmOpen] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const [viewOpen, setViewOpen] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const [bmOpen, setBmOpen] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const [kbdOpen, setKbdOpen] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const [searchOpen, setSearchOpen] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const [sheetWord, setSheetWord] = useState<Word | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [note, setNote] = useState<BookmarkNoteState | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [toast, setToast] = useState({ text: \\"\\", nonce: 0 });\\r\\n  const anyOpen = prefsOpen || wpmOpen || viewOpen || bmOpen || !!sheetWord || !!note || finished;\\r\\n  const kbdBtn = useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const changeSpeed = (wpm: number) => {\\r\\n    const v = clampWpm(wpm);\\r\\n    updatePrefs({ wpm: v });\\r\\n    setToast((t) => ({ text: `${v} WPM`, nonce: t.nonce + 1 }));\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"a06a40","wall_time_seconds":10.0043546,"session_id":20016,"original_token_count":20,"output":"\\n> vite_react_shadcn_ts@0.0.0 typecheck\\n> tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json\\n\\n"}'}]

SENTENCE_END = /[.!?][\\"\'’”)\\\\]]*$/;\\r\\nconst OTHER_PUNCT = /[,;:—–)\\\\]][\\"\'’”]*$/;\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Split text into paragraphs: one or more blank lines separate paragraphs. */\\r\\nexport function splitParagraphs(body: string): string[] {\\r\\n  return body\\r\\n    .replace(/\\\\r\\\\n/g, \\"\\\\n\\")\\r\\n    .split(/\\\\n\\\\s*\\\\n/)\\r\\n    .map((p) => p.trim())\\r\\n    .filter((p) => p.length > 0);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function tokenize(body: string): Token[] {\\r\\n  const tokens: Token[] = [];\\r\\n  const paragraphs = splitParagraphs(body);\\r\\n  paragraphs.forEach((para, paragraphIndex) => {\\r\\n    const words = para.split(/\\\\s+/).filter((w) => w.length > 0);\\r\\n    words.forEach((text, i) => {\\r\\n      const endsSentence = SENTENCE_END.test(text);\\r\\n      tokens.push({\\r\\n        index: tokens.length,\\r\\n        text,\\r\\n        paragraphIndex,\\r\\n        endsSentence,\\r\\n        hasPunctuation: !endsSentence && OTHER_PUNCT.test(text),\\r\\n        endsParagraph: i === words.length - 1,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  return tokens;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function countWords(body: string): number {\\r\\n  return tokenize(body).length;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Group tokens into chunks of up to wordsPerLine * numberOfLines words.\\r\\n * A chunk never crosses a paragraph boundary.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function chunkTokens(tokens: Token[], wordsPerLine: number, numberOfLines: number): Chunk[] {\\r\\n  const size = Math.max(1, wordsPerLine) * Math.max(1, numberOfLines);\\r\\n  const chunks: Chunk[] = [];\\r\\n  let current: Token[] = [];\\r\\n  const flush = () => {\\r\\n    if (current.length === 0) return;\\r\\n    const rows: Token[][] = [];\\r\\n    for (let i = 0; i < current.length; i += wordsPerLine) rows.push(current.slice(i, i + wordsPerLine));\\r\\n    chunks.push({\\r\\n      index: chunks.length,\\r\\n      startWord: current[0].index,\\r\\n      endWord: current[current.length - 1].index,\\r\\n      rows,\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    current = [];\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  for (const t of tokens) {\\r\\n    current.push(t);\\r\\n"}'}]

    </div>\\r\\n    </>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function PreferencesDialog({\\r\\n  open,\\r\\n  onClose,\\r\\n  prefs,\\r\\n  onChange,\\r\\n  name,\\r\\n  settingsOnly = false,\\r\\n}: public.has_reading_center_access() then raise exception \'Reading Center is not assigned\' using errcode=\'42501\'; end if;\\r\\n  if not exists(select 1 from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid() and name=p_old_name) then\\r\\n    raise exception \'Tag not found\' using errcode=\'22023\';\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n  insert into public.reading_center_tags(user_id,name,color) values(auth.uid(),trim(p_new_name),p_color)\\r\\n    on conflict(user_id,name) do update set color=excluded.color;\\r\\n  update public.reading_center_passage_metadata set tags=array(\\r\\n    select distinct x from unnest(array_replace(tags,p_old_name,trim(p_new_name))) as x\\r\\n  ) where user_id=auth.uid() and p_old_name=any(tags);\\r\\n  if p_old_name<>trim(p_new_name) then\\r\\n    delete from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid() and name=p_old_name;\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\nend;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\ncreate or replace function public.reading_center_delete_tag(p_name text)\\r\\nreturns void language plpgsql security invoker set search_path=public\\r\\nas $$\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n  if not public.has_reading_center_access() then raise exception \'Reading Center is not assigned\' using errcode=\'42501\'; end if;\\r\\n  update public.reading_center_passage_metadata set tags=array_remove(tags,p_name)\\r\\n    where user_id=auth.uid() and p_name=any(tags);\\r\\n [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 19.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"6abafc","wall_time_seconds":0.4773762,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":527,"output":"import React from \\"react\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Speedometer dial like Spreeder\'s Vital Stats gauges (0 to 2000): white face,\\r\\n * green arc, red needle, blue hub, value on the dial. Drawn from the live\\r\\n * screenshot; the original artwork was not extracted.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function SpeedGauge({ value, max = 2000, size = 84 }: { value: number; max?: number; size?: number }): React.ReactElement {\\r\\n  const start = 135; // degrees, 0 at 3 o\'clock, clockwise\\r\\n  const sweep = 270;\\r\\n  const pt = (deg: number, r: number) => {\\r\\n    const a = (deg * Math.PI) / 180;\\r\\n    return [50 + r * Math.cos(a), 50 + r * Math.sin(a)];\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  const arc = (r: number, from: number, to: number) => {\\r\\n    const [x1, y1] = pt(from, r);\\r\\n    const [x2, y2] = pt(to, r);\\r\\n    return `M ${x1} ${y1} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${to - from > 180 ? 1 : 0} 1 ${x2} ${y2}`;\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  const ratio = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, value / max));\\r\\n  const needle = start + sweep * ratio;\\r\\n  const [nx, ny] = pt(needle, 30);\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <svg viewBox=\\"0 0 100 100\\" width={size} height={size} role=\\"img\\" aria-label={`${value} words per minute`}>\\r\\n      <defs>\\r\\n        <radialGradient id=\\"rc-bezel\\" cx=\\"50%\\" cy=\\"40%\\" r=\\"60%\\">\\r\\n          <stop offset=\\"0%\\" stopColor=\\"#f5f5f5\\" />\\r\\n          <stop offset=\\"100%\\" stopColor=\\"#9e9e9e\\" />\\r\\n        </radialGradient>\\r\\n      </defs>\\r\\n      <circle cx=\\"50\\" cy=\\"50\\" r=\\"48\\" fill=\\"url(#rc-bezel)\\" />\\r\\n      <circle cx=\\"50\\" cy=\\"50\\" r=\\"42\\" fill=\\"#ffffff\\" />\\r\\n      <path d={arc(36, start, start + sweep)} fill=\\"none\\" stroke=\\"#2e9a2e\\" strokeWidth=\\"7\\" />\\r\\n      {Array.from({ length: 11 }, (_, i) => {\\r\\n        const d = start + (sweep * i) / 10;\\r\\n        const [x1, y1] = pt(d, 31);\\r\\n        const [x2, y2] = pt(d, 27);\\r\\n        return <line key={i} x1={x1} y1={y1} x2={x2} y2={y2} stroke=\\"#616161\\" strokeWidth=\\"1\\" />;\\r\\n      })}\\r\\n      <line x1=\\"50\\" y1=\\"50\\" x2={nx} y2={ny} stroke=\\"#e53935\\" strokeWidth=\\"3\\" strokeLinecap=\\"round\\" />\\r\\n      <circle cx=\\"50\\" cy=\\"50\\" r=\\"5\\" fill=\\"#3e92d7\\" />\\r\\n      <text x=\\"50\\" y=\\"78\\" textAnchor=\\"middle\\" fontSize=\\"9\\" fill=\\"#424242\\" fontFamily=\\"Poppins, sans-serif\\">{value}</text>\\r\\n    </svg>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"8b64fb","wall_time_seconds":0.3267994,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/src/features/reading-center/hooks/useReadingCenterData.test.tsx\n+import { act, renderHook, waitFor } from \"@testing-library/react\";\n+import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\n+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from \"vitest\";\n+import type { ReactNode } from \"react\";\n+import { usePassages, useSaveProgress, useUpdateBookmark } from \"./useReadingCenterData\";\n+\n+const state = vi.hoisted(() => ({ userId: \"student-a\", calls: [] as unknown[][], error: null as { message: string } | null, toast: vi.fn() }));\n+vi.mock(\"@/contexts/AuthContext\", () => ({ useAuth: () => ({ user: { id: state.userId } }) }));\n+vi.mock(\"sonner\", () => ({ toast: { error: state.toast } }));\n+\n+function request(table: string) {\n+  const builder: Record<string, unknown> = {};\n+  for (const method of [\"select\", \"order\", \"eq\", \"upsert\", \"update\", \"single\", \"maybeSingle\"]) {\n+    builder[method] = (...args: unknown[]) => { state.calls.push([table, method, ...args]); return builder; };\n+  }\n+  builder.then = (resolve: (result: unknown) => unknown) => Promise.resolve(resolve({ data: [], error: state.error }));\n+  return builder;\n+}\n+vi.mock(\"../lib/db\", () => ({ rcDb: (table: string) => request(table) }));\n+vi.mock(\"@/integrations/supabase/client\", () => ({ supabase: { from: (table: string) => request(table) } }));\n+\n+function harness() {\n+  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false }, mutations: { retry: false } } });\n+  return { client, wrapper: ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => <QueryClientProvider client={client}>{children}</QueryClientProvider> };\n+}\n+\n+beforeEach(() => { state.userId = \"student-a\"; state.calls = []; state.error = null; state.toast.mockClear(); });\n+\n+describe(\"Reading Center Supabase adapters\", () => {\n+  it(\"does not reuse the preceding student's cached library after an account switch\", async () => {\n+    const { client, wrapper } = harness();\n+    const hook = renderHook(() => usePassages(), { wrapper });\n+    await waitFor(() => expect(hook.result.current.isSuccess).toBe(true));\n+    state.userId = \"student-b\";\n+    hook.rerender();\n+    await waitFor(() => expect(client.getQueryState([\"reading-center\", \"student-b\", \"passages\", false])?.status).toBe(\"success\"));\n+    expect(state.calls).toContainEqual([\"reading_center_passage_metadata\", \"eq\", \"user_id\", \"student-b\"]);\n+    expect(client.getQueryData([\"reading-center\", \"student-a\", \"passages\", false])).toEqual([]);\n+  });\n+  it(\"rejects an RLS-filtered bookmark update instead of reporting it saved\", async () => {\n+    state.error = { message: \"Cannot coerce the result to a single JSON object\" };\n+    const { wrapper } = harness();\n+    const hook = renderHook(() => useUpdateBookmark(), { wrapper });\n+    await act(async () => { await expect(hook.result.current.mutateAsync({ id: \"other-account-bookmark\", note: \"Test\" })).rejects.toThrow(\"single JSON object\"); });\n+    expect(state.calls).toContainEqual([\"reading_center_bookmarks\", \"eq\", \"user_id\", \"student-a\"]);\n+    expect(state.calls).toContainEqual([\"reading_center_bookmarks\", \"single\"]);\n+    expect(state.toast).toHaveBeenCalled();\n+  });\n+  it(\"waits for confirmed completion and starts only the caller's assigned library item\", async () => {\n+    const { wrapper } = harness();\n+    const hook = renderHook(() => useSaveProgress(), { wrapper });\n+    await act(async () => { await hook.result.current.mutateAsync({ passage_id: \"shared\", word_index: 12, completed: true }); });\n+    expect(state.calls).toContainEqual([\"reading_center_progress\", \"single\"]);\n+    expect(state.calls).toContainEqual([\"classroom_items\", \"eq\", \"student_id\", \"student-a\"]);\n+    expect(state.calls).toContainEqual([\"classroom_items\", \"eq\", \"status\", \"assigned\"]);\n+    expect(state.calls).toContainEqual([\"classroom_items\", \"update\", { status: \"in_progress\" }]);\n+    expect(state.calls.some((call) => call[0] === \"classroom_items\" && call[1] === \"update\" && (call[2] as { status: string }).status === \"completed\")).toBe(false);\n+  });\n+});\n*** End Patch"));

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 16.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"7c06cd","wall_time_seconds":0.5108172,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":427,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Timer in a Web Worker, as Spreeder does it: browsers clamp setTimeout in\\r\\n * background or hidden tabs to once a second, which would freeze reading at\\r\\n * 60 WPM. Worker timers are not clamped. Falls back to setTimeout.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst SOURCE = `\\r\\nlet id = null;\\r\\nself.onmessage = (e) => {\\r\\n  if (id !== null) { clearTimeout(id); id = null; }\\r\\n  if (e.data && e.data.delay != null) {\\r\\n    const start = Date.now();\\r\\n    const tick = () => {\\r\\n      if (Date.now() - start >= e.data.delay) { id = null; self.postMessage(e.data.token); }\\r\\n      else id = setTimeout(tick, Math.min(4, e.data.delay));\\r\\n    };\\r\\n    id = setTimeout(tick, Math.max(0, e.data.delay - 4));\\r\\n  }\\r\\n};`;\\r\\n\\r\\nlet worker: Worker | null | undefined;\\r\\nlet nextToken = 1;\\r\\nconst callbacks = new Map<number, () => void>();\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction getWorker(): Worker | null {\\r\\n  if (worker !== undefined) return worker;\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    worker = new Worker(URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([SOURCE], { type: \\"text/javascript\\" })));\\r\\n    worker.onmessage = (e: MessageEvent<number>) => {\\r\\n      const cb = callbacks.get(e.data);\\r\\n      callbacks.delete(e.data);\\r\\n      cb?.();\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    worker = null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return worker;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** One pending timer at a time (the reader only ever needs one). Returns a cancel function. */\\r\\nexport function startTimer(delay: number, cb: () => void): () => void {\\r\\n  const w = getWorker();\\r\\n  if (!w) {\\r\\n    const t = window.setTimeout(cb, delay);\\r\\n    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const token = nextToken++;\\r\\n  callbacks.clear();\\r\\n  callbacks.set(token, cb);\\r\\n  w.postMessage({ delay, token });\\r\\n  return () => {\\r\\n    if (callbacks.delete(token)) w.postMessage({ delay: null });\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"4da955","wall_time_seconds":0.5770247,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":497,"output":" M package-lock.json\\n M package.json\\n M src/features/reading-center/components/PassageEditor.tsx\\n M src/features/reading-center/components/ViewPanel.tsx\\n M src/features/reading-center/hooks/useReadingCenterData.ts\\n M src/features/reading-center/lib/routes.ts\\n M src/features/reading-center/lib/types.ts\\n M src/features/reading-center/pages/AdminPassagesPage.tsx\\n M src/features/reading-center/pages/LibraryPage.tsx\\n M src/features/reading-center/pages/ReaderPage.tsx\\n?? [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 16.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"6e2a87","wall_time_seconds":0.6347546,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1135,"output":"import React, { useCallback, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport type { Preferences } from \\"../lib/types\\";\\r\\nimport type { Word } from \\"../lib/spreeder\\";\\r\\nimport { findMatches } from \\"./ViewPanel\\";\\r\\nimport { Icon, MIcon } from \\"./Icon\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nconst GAP = 48;\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Spreeder e-Reader: the passage as one stream in a ~734px column, paged with\\r\\n * CSS columns. Words turn #3e92d7 on hover and open word actions on click.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function EReader({\\r\\n  prefs,\\r\\n  fontStack,\\r\\n  words,\\r\\n  page,\\r\\n  onPageCount,\\r\\n  onPageWord,\\r\\n  highlight,\\r\\n  onWordClick,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  prefs: Preferences;\\r\\n  fontStack: string;\\r\\n  words: Word[];\\r\\n  page: number;\\r\\n  onPageCount: (n: number) => void;\\r\\n  /** First word index on the visible page (reading position). */\\r\\n  onPageWord: (i: number, endExclusive: number) => void;\\r\\n  highlight: { start: number; length: number }[];\\r\\n  onWordClick: (w: Word) => void;\\r\\n}): React.ReactElement {\\r\\n  const box = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [width, setWidth] = useState(0);\\r\\n\\r\\n  useLayoutEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const el = box.current;\\r\\n    if (!el) return;\\r\\n    const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => setWidth(el.clientWidth));\\r\\n    ro.observe(el);\\r\\n    setWidth(el.clientWidth);\\r\\n    return () => ro.disconnect();\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  useLayoutEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const el = box.current;\\r\\n    if (!el || width <= 0) return;\\r\\n    onPageCount(Math.max(1, Math.round((el.scrollWidth + GAP) / (width + GAP))));\\r\\n  }, [width, words, prefs.font_family, prefs.font_size, prefs.line_spacing, onPageCount]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  useLayoutEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const el = box.current;\\r\\n    if (!el || width <= 0) return;\\r\\n    el.scrollLeft = page * (width + GAP);\\r\\n    const spans = [...el.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(\\"[data-word]\\")];\\r\\n    const first = spans.find((s) => s.offsetLeft >= page * (width + GAP) - 1);\\r\\n    const after = spans.find((s) => s.offsetLeft >= (page + 1) * (width + GAP) - 1);\\r\\n    if (first) onPageWord(Number(first.dataset.word), after ?
